
#Please use the same values found in "POD_domicile_values" for Fera domiciles
@fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t1 = 180 
@fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t2 = 300
@fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t3 = 420
@fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t4 = 540
@fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t5 = 660
@fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t6 = 780

#Internals are 75% the time of the externals
@fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t1 = @[fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t1 * 0.75]
@fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t2 = @[fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t2 * 0.75]
@fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t3 = @[fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t3 * 0.75]
@fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t4 = @[fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t4 * 0.75]
@fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t5 = @[fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t5 * 0.75]
@fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t6 = @[fera_domicile_external_construction_duration_t6 * 0.75]


#######################
#### Main Building ####
#######################
#AI only uses the dragon obelisk, rest are too complex

mokole_main_meditation_chamber_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t1
	previous_building = camp_main_01

	cost = {
		gold = fera_domicile_internal_gold_cost_t1
		piety = fera_domicile_internal_piety_cost_t1
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = fera_domicile_internal_gold_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = fera_domicile_internal_piety_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_mokole = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = saved_haven_buildings target = flag:mokole_main_meditation_chamber_01 } } }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = mokole_main_meditation_chamber_01_requirement
				capital_province = { has_building = mokole_wallow_05 }
			}
		}
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_mokole = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		domicile_gnosis_regen_2_5 = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		POD_terrain_gnosis_regen_supply_limit_mult = 0.025
		stress_loss_mult = 0.05
	}

	ai_value = { value = fera_domicile_ai_never }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_ascetics.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_baggage_train.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/laamp_building_baggage_train_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/camp_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_baggage_train"
	}
}

mokole_main_dragon_obelisk_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @fera_domicile_internal_construction_duration_t1
	previous_building = camp_main_01

	cost = {
		gold = fera_domicile_internal_gold_cost_t1
		piety = fera_domicile_internal_piety_cost_t1
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = fera_domicile_internal_gold_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		piety = {
			value = fera_domicile_internal_piety_cost_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_mokole = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = saved_haven_buildings target = flag:mokole_main_dragon_obelisk_01 } } }
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = mokole_main_dragon_obelisk_01_requirement
				POD_fera_rank_value >= 5
			}
		}
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_mokole = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		
		archid_cost_warning = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		POD_terrain_mokole_archid_cost_advantage = -0.5
	}

	ai_value = { value = fera_domicile_ai_always }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_watchtower.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_watchtower_mena.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_watchtower_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_watchtower"
	}
}
